Addressing a significant gap in Arabic lexical infrastructure, the study adopts a dual-standard framing that aligns the ISO Lexical Markup Framework (LMF) with the Text Encoding Initiative TEI Lex-0 guidelines. By applying an editorial view to the dictionary's macro- and microstructure, the research resolves the structural ambiguities and punctuation inconsistencies typical of 20th-century bilingual dictionaries. The methodology is grounded in an empirical analysis of the dictionary's lexical knowledge density. Drawing on a representative sample (the letter Ayn, comprising 4.6% of the total volume), the study provides scientific weight to the encoding process, demonstrating a structural parsing accuracy of 91%. Quantitative evaluation of the information extraction rules reveals high performance, with 85% precision and 98% recall for synonyms, and 88% precision for other morpho-semantic features. Beyond technical description, the paper provides a critical comparison with existing Arabic lexical resources and discusses the limitations of TEI Lex-0 when modelling specific Arabic phenomena, such as implicit "open set" semantic relations and scattered morphological cues. Furthermore, the study explores the potential for Linguistic Linked Open Data (LLOD) integration by establishing a scalable prefix-based referencing system that facilitates the resource's inclusion in the semantic web. The result is an interoperable, machine-tractable resource that provides a reproducible workflow for the retro-digitization of complex legacy bilingual lexicons within the Arabic NLP and Digital Humanities communities.

</details>


### [138] [Zone of Proximal Policy Optimization: Teacher in Prompts, Not Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18216)
*Byung-Kwan Lee,Ximing Lu,Shizhe Diao,Minki Kang,Saurav Muralidharan,Karan Sapra,Andrew Tao,Pavlo Molchanov,Yejin Choi,Yu-Chiang Frank Wang,Ryo Hachiuma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ZPPO 借鉴维果茨基"最近发展区"理论，在提示层面（而非策略梯度层面）引入教师信号：将难题重构为二元候选问题（BCQ）和负候选问题（NCQ），结合提示回放缓冲区，在学生的最近发展区内训练，在小模型上显著优于离/在线蒸馏和 GRPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识蒸馏在小模型场景下存在脆弱性：强迫小学生模仿大教师的 logits 会使其过度集中在教师的最锐利模式上，损害泛化能力。强化学习虽然避免了 logit 模仿，但在难题上学生所有 rollout 都失败时（零优势并被静默丢弃），注入教师响应会破坏 on-policy 假设并导致策略漂移。需要一种在不破坏强化学习假设的前提下有效利用教师信号的方法。

Method: 提出 ZPPO（Zone of Proximal Policy Optimization）：(1) 教师保持在提示中而非策略梯度中；(2) 对难题构建两种重构提示——BCQ（将一个正确教师响应与一个错误学生响应匿名配对，让学生判别）和 NCQ（聚合学生错误 rollout 暴露共同失败模式）；(3) 提示回放缓冲区循环回放每道难题，直到学生平均 rollout 准确率达 50% 毕业，或按 FIFO 逐出，从而在学生当前最近发展区内放大 BCQ 和 NCQ 训练信号。

Result: 在 Qwen3.5 系列四个学生规模（0.8B-9B）上，以 27B 教师进行视觉语言模型后训练，在 31 个基准（16 VLM、10 LLM、5 Video）上评估，ZPPO 全面优于离/在线蒸馏和 GRPO，且最小规模（0.8B）的收益最大。

Conclusion: ZPPO 通过在提示空间而非策略梯度空间引入教师引导，解决了小模型强化学习中的难题零优势问题，实现了比传统蒸馏和 GRPO 更优的小模型训练效果，尤其在小规模模型上优势显著，验证了"最近发展区"理论在策略优化中的有效性。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation transfers a teacher's competence to a small student but is brittle in the small-student regime: forcing the student to imitate logits from a much larger teacher concentrates it on the teacher's sharpest modes, hurting generalization on benchmark families beyond the training corpus. Reinforcement learning (RL) avoids logit imitation by training on the student's own rollouts. However, on questions where every rollout fails-yielding zero advantage and being silently discarded-injecting a stronger teacher's response into the policy gradient breaks the on-policy assumption and induces drift. We introduce Zone of Proximal Policy Optimization (ZPPO), inspired by Vygotsky's zone of proximal development, which keeps the teacher inside the prompt rather than the policy gradient. On hard questions, ZPPO constructs two reformulated prompts: a Binary Candidate-included Question (BCQ) pairs one correct teacher response with one incorrect student response as anonymized candidates the student must discriminate, and a Negative Candidate-included Question (NCQ) aggregates the student's wrong rollouts into a single prompt to surface their shared failure modes. A prompt replay buffer recirculates each hard question until it either graduates-the student's mean rollout accuracy on it reaches half- or is FIFO-evicted under finite capacity, amplifying BCQ and NCQ inside the student's current zone of proximal development. On the Qwen3.5 family at four student scales (0.8B-9B) with a 27B teacher, post-trained as vision-language models and evaluated on a 31-benchmark suite (16 VLM, 10 LLM, 5 Video), ZPPO outperforms off/on-policy distillation and GRPO, with the largest gains at the smallest scale.

</details>


### [139] [Darshana Graph: A Parallel Commentary Corpus for Comparative Indian Philosophy, with Stylometric and Exploratory Graph Analyses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18222)
*Joy Bose*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 介绍Darshana Graph语料库（12.5万+条印度哲学文本），其核心特色是约8500条跨18位注释者的对齐数据。论文进行了两项分析：无机器学习的风格计量比较，以及基于约束大语言模型管道的哲学关系抽取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏大规模、公开可用的跨注释者对齐的印度哲学语料库，使得研究者无法系统性地比较不同解释传统如何解读同一经典文本。该语料库旨在填补这一空白，支持对印度哲学传统（特别是吠檀多各派）的定量比较研究。

Method: （1）构建Darshana Graph语料库，收录12.5万+条文本记录，核心为约8500条同一根偈/经文在18位注释者间的对齐数据；（2）透明风格计量分析：通过经文引用密度、显性反驳率和句子复杂度三个指标，无需机器学习即可测量论证风格；（3）约束LLM管道：使用预定义关系词汇和确定性后验验证，抽取概念间的类型化哲学关系，并构建关系图。

Result: 风格计量分析发现：引用密度与反驳率呈中等负相关；三个同属一条教义谱系的注释者反驳率显著递增；巴利藏内部存在可测量的体裁级差异。LLM关系抽取揭示了跨学派的分歧模式，但也暴露出抽取局限性——独立嵌入分析与图衍生分析在某些情况下结论相左。

Conclusion: Darshana Graph语料库为印度哲学的计算研究提供了独特资源，其跨注释者对齐结构使直接比较不同解释传统成为可能。风格计量方法展示了透明、可解释的定量分析路径，而约束LLM管道虽有效但也需谨慎对待其局限性。语料库、关系图和代码全部开源。

Abstract: We introduce Darshana Graph, a corpus of over 125,000 text records spanning classical Hindu, Buddhist, and Jain philosophical traditions, drawn from public-domain and openly licensed translations of sources including the Bhagavad Gita, Brahma Sutras, principal Upanishads, the Pali Canon, and core Jain texts. Its distinctive contribution lies in a structurally unique subset of roughly 8,500 Hindu and Jain records in which the same root verse or sutra is aligned across eighteen historical commentators representing five schools of Vedanta and other darshanas, enabling direct comparison of how independent interpretive traditions read identical source material. To our knowledge, no publicly available resource provides comparable cross-commentator alignment at this scale. We present two analyses built on this corpus. First, a transparent stylometric comparison requiring no machine learning measures argumentative style through scriptural citation density, explicit refutation rate, and sentence complexity. It finds a moderate negative correlation between citation density and refutation rate, a marked increase in refutation rate across three commentators in a related doctrinal lineage, and measurable genre-level differences within the Pali Canon itself. Second, we describe a constrained large language model pipeline that extracts typed philosophical relationships between concepts using a predefined relation vocabulary and deterministic post-hoc validation. The resulting graph surfaces cross-school disagreement patterns while also revealing important extraction limitations, including cases where an independent embedding-based analysis disagrees with the graph-derived findings. We release the full corpus, extracted relationship graph, and all source code.

</details>


### [140] [ReproRepo: Scaling Reproducibility Audits with GitHub Repository Issues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18237)
*Shanda Li,Qiuhong Anna Wei,Jingwu Tang,Valerie Chen,Nihar B Shah,Tim Dettmers,Yiming Yang,Ameet Talwalkar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ReproRepo 是一个可扩展的框架，利用 GitHub 上人工提出的 issue 作为自然监督信号，评估 LLM 智能体在真实世界可复现性问题审计上的表现。在 1,149 篇近期机器学习论文上的实验表明，最佳智能体（Codex + GPT-5.5）能为约 90% 的论文发现至少一个与人工报告语义相关的复现障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 LLM 可复现性评估基准依赖大量人工进行数据整理和评估，难以规模化扩展。论文旨在提出一个可扩展的自动化框架来解决这一瓶颈。

Method: 提出 ReproRepo 框架，以 GitHub issue 作为自然发生的监督信号来评估复现障碍。具体做法：收集 1,149 篇近期顶会机器学习论文及其代码仓库，将人工提交的 GitHub issue 作为 ground truth，评估四种前沿模型-智能体配置在不执行代码的情况下，从论文-仓库对中识别可复现性问题的能力。

Result: 最佳智能体 Codex + GPT-5.5 在约 90% 的论文中至少发现一个与人工报告语义相关的复现阻塞点。智能体在发现显性失败和识别正确语义区域方面表现突出，但在精确问题定位方面仍存在不足。

Conclusion: ReproRepo 为未来 LLM 智能体在真实世界可复现性审计方面的评估提供了一个可复用、可扩展的框架。LLM 智能体虽展现出强大的复现问题发现能力，但精确诊断能力仍有提升空间。

Abstract: Reproducing research results from papers and released code is central to scientific progress. Existing works have introduced benchmarks to evaluate whether LLM agents can assist with reproducibility, but they are difficult to scale due to their reliance on substantial manual effort for data curation and evaluation. We introduce ReproRepo, a scalable framework for reproducibility evaluation that leverages human-raised GitHub issues as naturally occurring supervision on realistic reproduction blockers. We instantiate ReproRepo on 1,149 recent machine learning papers from major conferences and evaluate four frontier model-agent configurations. Our results show that LLM agents, even without executing code, can identify many real-world reproducibility problems from paper-repository pairs: the best agent in our study, namely Codex with GPT-5.5, surfaces at least one semantically related human-reported blocker for ~90% of papers in the study. Further analysis shows that agents are particularly effective for surfacing visible failures and identifying the right semantic region, but may still be insufficient in exact localization. ReproRepo can serve as a reusable, scalable framework for future evaluations of LLM agents on real-world reproducibility auditing. Our code is released at https://github.com/LithiumDA/ReproRepo.

</details>


### [141] [Variable-Width Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18246)
*Zhaofeng Wu,Oliver Sieberling,Shawn Tan,Rameswar Panda,Yury Polyanskiy,Yoon Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"> <former"（沙漏型/×形）的Transformer架构，通过在不同深度层之间进行非均匀宽度分配——即早期和后期层较宽、中间层较窄——在保持参数匹配的前提下，相比均匀宽度的基线模型取得了更优的语言建模性能，同时减少了计算量和KV缓存开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前主流Transformer架构在所有层之间保持恒定宽度，均等分配参数和计算预算。然而，不同深度的层可能承担不同的计算角色，这种"一刀切"的均匀分配方式可能并非最优。作者旨在探索通过非均匀容量分配来实现更资源高效的模型扩展。

Method: 提出"> <former"架构：加宽早期（靠近输入）和后期（靠近输出）层的宽度，同时收窄中间层，形成沙漏/×形结构。采用无参数的残差尺寸调整机制（residual resizing mechanism）来连接不同宽度的层。在200M到2B参数（稠密模型）及3B参数（MoE模型）规模的decoder-only语言模型上进行验证。

Result: 在参数匹配的条件下，> <former在语言建模损失上一致优于均匀宽度的基线模型。平均层宽度降低带来了22%的FLOPs减少（在拟合损失匹配的扩展曲线下）以及15%的KV缓存内存和I/O成本降低。分析表明，瓶颈结构导致残差流中产生定性不同的表征。

Conclusion: 非均匀宽度分配可以实现更具资源最优性的语言模型扩展，为Transformer架构设计提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Scaling model size, specifically depth and width, has driven significant progress in transformer-based language models. However, most architectures maintain a constant width across all layers, allocating a fixed parameter and computation budget evenly despite different layers potentially playing distinct computational roles. In this work, we empirically investigate nonuniform capacity allocation across network depth by proposing a $\times$-shaped > <former architecture. This design maintains wider early and late layers while narrowing the middle layers, utilizing a parameter-free residual resizing mechanism. Across decoder-only language models ranging from 200M to 2B parameters (dense) and 3B parameters (MoE), our > <former consistently outperforms parameter-matched uniform baselines on language modeling loss. By reducing the average layer width, this architecture also requires fewer overall FLOPs (22% reduction under fitted loss-matched scaling curves) and smaller KV cache memory and I/O cost (15% reduction). In analysis, we show that this bottleneck structure results in qualitatively different representations in residual streams. Overall, our results demonstrate that nonuniform width allocation can result in more resource-optimal scaling of language models.

</details>


<div id='stat.ME'></div>

# stat.ME [[Back]](#toc)

### [142] [Statistical Foundations of LLM-based A/B Testing: A Surrogacy Framework for Human Causal Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17165)
*Joel Persson,Mårten Schultzberg,Sebastian Ankargren*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个统计框架，将替代终点理论应用于大语言模型（LLM），用于判断LLM在A/B测试中替代人类参与者的有效性。研究表明，通过将LLM结果校准到人类结果，在比分布等价更弱的条件下即可识别平均处理效应；当条件不满足时，可借助诊断工具和历史实验进行证伪，并给出有限重叠下的最坏偏差边界。核心结论是：LLM替代的有效性只能对过去处理进行证伪，无法对新处理进行验证，因此人类实验对新型干预仍不可或缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 越来越多的组织和研究者希望用LLM替代人类参与者进行A/B测试，以降低实验成本、加快实验速度。然而，LLM结果与人类结果之间何时可以互相替代并不清楚。简单要求两者分布等价既不现实也过于严格，因此需要一套更精细的统计框架来判断LLM作为人类替代的适用条件与局限性。

Method: 作者将替代终点理论（surrogate endpoint theory）适配到LLM场景，提出了一个统计框架。该框架定义了替代性（surrogacy）和可比性（comparability）两个核心条件，这两者联合弱于分布等价，但在满足时可识别平均处理效应（ATE）。当条件不满足时，效应仅能部分识别，作者提供了基于历史实验的证伪诊断工具，以及因重叠有限导致的最坏偏差边界。此外，针对LLM固有的随机性，证明了使用多次抽取的平均值作为替代指标可同时降低偏差和方差。方法通过模拟和Upworthy标题A/B测试的实证应用进行了验证。

Result: （1）在替代性与可比性条件下，LLM校准后可一致估计ATE；（2）当条件不成立时，效应只能部分识别，并给出了诊断和偏差边界；（3）LLM的随机性会引入偏差和方差，但多次抽取取平均可缓解两者；（4）实证应用验证了方法的可行性。核心发现：LLM替代有效性可被证伪（针对历史处理），但永远无法被验证（针对新处理），因此人类实验对新型干预依然必不可少。

Conclusion: LLM作为A/B测试中人类替代品的有效性取决于替代性和可比性条件是否满足，这些条件可通过历史实验进行证伪但无法验证。LLM选择、提示设计和温度等是重要的设计变量，且需要人类实验来确定验证所需的样本量。对于全新干预，人类实验仍然不可替代。

Abstract: Organizations and researchers show increasing interest in using large language models (LLMs) in place of human participants in A/B tests, in the hope of experimenting faster and at lower cost. We study when a treatment effect estimated on LLM outcomes recovers the effect that would have been measured on the human population of interest. Distributional equivalence between LLM and human outcomes would make any standard estimator valid but is unrealistic. We therefore develop a statistical framework that adapts surrogate endpoint theory to LLMs. The framework shows that calibrating LLM outcomes to human outcomes identifies the average treatment effect under surrogacy and comparability conditions that are jointly weaker than distributional equivalence. When these conditions fail, the effect of interest is only partially identified, and we provide diagnostics that can falsify surrogacy on historical experiments together with a bound on the worst-case bias from limited overlap. We further show that the stochasticity inherent to LLMs introduces both bias and variance, but using an average of multiple draws as the surrogate mitigates both. We illustrate the methods and theory in simulations and an application to A/B tests on Upworthy headlines. A central takeaway from our work is that the validity of LLM outcomes as surrogates can only be falsified for past treatments and never verified for new ones, so human experiments remain indispensable for novel interventions. We discuss the role of LLM choice, prompting, and temperature as design variables, and how to size human experiments for validation.

</details>


### [143] [Tropical Viterbi Tubes for Decoding Uncertainty in Hidden Markov Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17181)
*Aurélien Nicosia*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 该论文提出了"热带Viterbi管"（tropical Viterbi tube）——在拟合的隐马尔可夫模型中，与Viterbi最优路径的完整数据对数得分差距在容差范围内的所有隐藏状态轨迹的集合。通过对状态、转移和变化状态进行投影，可以揭示哪些局部特征与全局近优路径兼容，从而为HMM解码提供路径级不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Viterbi解码仅报告单一的最优隐藏状态路径，忽略了多个近优轨迹带来的路径不确定性。当解码状态具有科学意义时（如生态行为标注、金融状态识别、生物医学监测等），这种"唯一最优"会掩盖推断的可变性，作者希望构建一种表征全局近优路径集合的方法。

Method: 核心方法分三步：(1) 将"热带Viterbi管"定义为完整隐藏路径空间上的后验超水平集，容差被解释为相对于Viterbi路径的对数后验优势损失；(2) 证明管的单调性、阶跃函数行为和确定性稳定保证，并通过max-plus前向-后向递推在O(TK²)时间内精确计算投影管（稠密转移矩阵情形）；(3) 通过容差校准至目标后验质量得到HPD阈值可信区域及保守同步投影带，后验管质量和HPD校准由FFBS（前向滤波后向抽样）近似的单独路径计算完成。

Result: 在公开的蝙蝠追踪数据上验证：稳健觅食管段对取食嗡声显著富集（η=0.005时，富集比2.25，95%自助置信区间(1.73, 2.85)），而稳健通勤段则显著贫化（富集比0.27，置信区间(0.16, 0.44)），表明该方法能有效分离出高置信的行为状态段。

Conclusion: 热带Viterbi管为HMM提供了一层路径级不确定性量化，使研究者能够识别在近优全局路径中保持稳定的局部特征，适用于序列分析、生态学、金融、生物医学监测等多个领域。

Abstract: Hidden Markov models are widely used to infer latent state sequences from sequential data, but Viterbi decoding reports only one most likely complete path. When decoded states carry scientific meaning, this single maximizer can conceal pathwise uncertainty created by multiple near-optimal trajectories. Conditional on a fitted HMM, we introduce the tropical Viterbi tube: the set of hidden trajectories whose complete-data log-score lies within a tolerance of the Viterbi optimum. State, transition, and change-status projections show which local features remain compatible with globally near-optimal complete paths, giving a pathwise uncertainty layer for HMMs in sequence analysis, ecology, finance, biomedical monitoring, and related domains.
  The tube is a posterior superlevel set on complete hidden-path space, with tolerance interpreted as a log posterior-odds loss relative to a Viterbi path. Calibrating the tolerance to a target posterior mass gives an HPD-threshold credible region for the complete latent path and conservative simultaneous projected bands. We prove monotonicity, step-function behavior, and deterministic stability guarantees, and compute projected tubes exactly by max-plus forward-backward recursions in O(TK^2) time for dense transitions. Posterior tube mass and HPD calibration are separate pathwise calculations approximated by FFBS. In a public bat-tracking application, robust foraging tube segments are enriched for feeding buzzes, whereas robust commuting segments are depleted: at eta = 0.005, enrichment is 2.25 with 95% bootstrap interval (1.73, 2.85) for robust foraging and 0.27 with interval (0.16, 0.44) for robust commuting.

</details>


### [144] [Semiparametric Mediation Analysis with Separately Observed Mediator and Outcome under Unmeasured Confounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17232)
*Sijia Li,Ruoyu Wang*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新颖的数据融合框架，通过结合两个分别测量中介变量M和结局Y的不完整数据源，利用共享工具变量恢复自然直接和间接效应的识别，并在多个稳健性和效率条件下发展了半参数估计量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大量现实研究中，中介变量M和结局Y从未被联合观测，导致标准的中介效应识别策略失效。需要一种方法能够在不完整数据条件下恢复因果路径的识别。

Method: 提出基于共享工具变量（IVs）的数据融合框架，在无交互作用条件和潜在对齐条件下，解决M和Y未联合观测的问题。建立两种识别策略：已知有效IVs的情形和需从数据中学习有效IVs的情形。进一步开发了基于影响函数的半参数估计量，具备多重稳健性，并在适当条件下达到半参数效率界。

Result: 在适当条件下，所提出的估计量具有多重稳健性，并能达到半参数效率界。框架被应用于量化SNP rs610932通过免疫相关基因表达通路对痴呆风险的中介效应。

Conclusion: 该数据融合框架成功解决了M和Y未被联合观测时的中介分析识别难题，为利用不完整数据源进行因果路径分解提供了可行且高效的统计方法。

Abstract: Mediation analysis is widely used to disentangle causal pathways, yet in many real-world studies the mediator M and outcome Y are never jointly observed. This incompleteness breaks the standard identification strategy for natural direct and indirect effects. We introduce a novel data fusion framework that restores the identification by combining two incomplete data sources, one measuring $M$ and the other measuring Y. Our approach leverages shared instrumental variables (IVs) to circumvent the need to observe (M,Y) jointly, remains valid under unmeasured confounding via a no-interaction condition, and accommodates covariate and exposure shifts across data sources under a latent alignment condition. We establish two identification strategies, one for settings with a known set of valid IVs, and another for settings where valid IVs must be learned. We further develop semiparametric, influence-function-based estimators with multiple robustness properties, and propose an estimator that attains the semiparametric efficiency bound under appropriate conditions. We apply our framework to quantify the extent to which the effect of SNP rs610932 on dementia risk is mediated through immune-related gene-expression pathways.

</details>


### [145] [Bayesian Poisson-Randomized Gamma Tensor Factorization with Application to International Trade Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17267)
*Jie Jian,Aaron Schein*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种贝叶斯分层张量因子分解模型，用于处理稀疏半连续张量数据（如国际贸易流量），该数据具有过量零值、重右尾和切片特异性离散特征。模型通过低秩CP结构建模潜在泊松率张量，并结合条件Gamma模型处理正值，采用混合变分-蒙特卡洛算法实现大规模推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 国际贸易等货币价值型多维数据具有三个典型特征：(1) 大多数出口商-进口商-产品-年份单元格为零值；(2) 正值连续且高度可变，呈现重尾分布；(3) 不同切片（如不同年份）的离散程度差异显著。现有方法（如引力模型或成对网络分析）无法联合建模产品和时间维度，难以捕捉多维依赖结构。

Method: 提出贝叶斯分层张量因子分解模型：(1) 在潜在泊松率张量上施加低秩CP结构，建模零值发生概率；(2) 对正值采用条件Gamma模型，其率参数可在某一模态的切片间变化，以捕捉切片特异性离散；(3) 通过共享低秩潜在结构在所有张量维度间借力。为扩展至大规模数据，开发了混合变分-蒙特卡洛算法，结合坐标上升更新与部分塌缩增广数据采样器。

Result: 在约6000万条贸易流数据上应用该方法，成功揭示了出口商、进口商、产品和年份之间的多向依赖结构，这种结构难以通过引力模型或成对网络分析等方法恢复。

Conclusion: 该模型有效分离了正观测值的发生与量级两个过程，同时通过共享低秩结构实现跨维度信息借用。混合变分-蒙特卡洛推断算法使模型可扩展至千万级数据规模，为大规模稀疏半连续张量数据提供了实用的分析框架。

Abstract: We study sparse semi-continuous tensor data with excess zeros, heavy right tails, and slice-specific dispersion. Such features arise naturally in monetary-valued multi-way data, such as international trade, where most exporter--importer--product--year cells are zero while positive values are continuous and highly variable. To model these data, we propose a Bayesian hierarchical tensor factorization model that places a low-rank CP structure on a latent Poisson rate tensor and couples it with a conditional Gamma model for positive outcomes, with rate parameters that can vary across slices within a mode. The model therefore separates the occurrence and magnitude of positive observations while borrowing strength across all tensor dimensions through a shared low-rank latent structure. To scale posterior inference to large arrays, we develop a hybrid variational--Monte Carlo algorithm that combines efficient coordinate ascent updates with a partially collapsed augmented-data sampler. Applied to approximately 60 million trade flows, the method surfaces multiway dependence across exporters, importers, products, and years that is difficult to recover from gravity-type or pairwise network analyses, which do not jointly model the product and temporal dimensions.

</details>


### [146] [Dependent Censoring Based on Geometric Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17293)
*Anis Fradi,Salima Helali,Bilel Bousselmi*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出基于扩展广义Marshall-Olkin (EGMO) 模型的框架，利用几何优化技术处理生存分析中的相依删失问题，并建立了估计量的渐近性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生存分析中，相依删失（failure time与censoring time存在统计依赖）会严重干扰模型参数和生存函数的准确估计，传统独立删失假设下方法失效，故需发展能刻画并处理相依删失机制的新框架。

Method: 引入扩展广义Marshall-Olkin (EGMO) 模型来建模失效时间与删失时间之间的相依结构；采用几何优化技术构造有效的估计程序，以捕捉两者的依赖关系；并推导估计量的渐近性质。

Result: 模拟研究验证了所提方法的稳健性，实际数据分析进一步证明了其有效性。

Conclusion: EGMO模型结合几何优化为相依删失问题提供了一种可行且有效的解决方案，在理论和应用层面均表现良好。

Abstract: In survival analysis, dependent censoring poses significant challenges in accurately estimating model parameters and survival functions. This study introduces a novel framework leveraging Extended Generalized Marshall-Olkin (EGMO) models to address dependent censoring mechanisms. Geometric optimization techniques are employed to develop efficient estimation procedures that capture dependencies between failure and censoring times. We establish their asymptotic properties. Simulation studies and real data applications illustrate the method's robustness and effectiveness.

</details>


### [147] [Kernel-Based Functional Balancing for Causal Inference with Compositional Treatments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17308)
*Sungbum Kim,Jiayi Wang*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出一种处理组合型处理（compositional treatments）的因果效应估计方法，通过核协变量函数平衡构建权重，并结合核岭回归与边际增广提出增广加权估计量（AWE），实现了√n一致性且无需权重的一致估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统因果推断中处理变量多为标量或二值，但现实中存在大量组合型处理（如营养成分比例、时间分配比例），其取值位于单纯形上。现有方法难以直接处理此类处理变量的因果效应估计问题，需要在组合空间上定义并估计因果效应。

Method: 1. 将平均潜在结果投影到处理空间，定义组合型处理的因果估计目标；2. 在处理与协变量的联合空间上构建再生核希尔伯特空间（RKHS），通过直接最小化最坏情况平衡误差获得权重，而非依赖处理分配模型；3. 提出增广加权估计量（AWE），利用核岭回归估计结果函数，并结合协变量分布的边际增广；4. 通过表示定理和低秩近似将目标转化为有限维凸优化问题求解。

Result: 所提出的AWE估计量实现了√n一致性，且不要求权重的一致估计或光滑性；建立了围绕样本特定目标的渐近正态性；模拟研究和实际数据应用验证了其良好的经验性能。

Conclusion: 该方法为组合型处理的因果效应估计提供了一个理论严谨且实用的框架，突破了传统方法对处理变量形式的限制，在营养学、时间利用等领域具有广泛应用前景。

Abstract: We study causal effect estimation with compositional treatments, where the exposure lies on a simplex and the estimand is defined over compositions rather than scalar or binary values. By considering a projection of the average potential outcome onto the treatment space, a kernel-based covariate functional balancing approach is adopted for weight construction. The weights are obtained by directly minimizing a worst-case balancing error over a reproducing kernel Hilbert space (RKHS) defined on the joint space of treatments and covariates, instead of being estimated under a treatment assignment model. Building on these weights, an augmented weighted estimator (AWE) is proposed, where the outcome function is estimated via kernel ridge regression and combined with a marginal augmentation over the covariate distribution. Despite the complex structure of the resulting objective, a finite-dimensional convex optimization problem is formulated via a representer theorem and a low-rank approximation. The proposed estimator achieves $\sqrt{n}$-consistency without requiring consistent estimation or smoothness of the weights. An asymptotic normality result is established around a sample-specific target. Empirical performance is demonstrated through simulation studies and a real data application.

</details>


### [148] [The dangers of using three-number summaries to estimate unknown standard deviations: sensitivity analyses and some possible improvements incorporating shape](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17424)
*Udara Kumaranathunga,Alysha De Livera,Luke A. Prendergast*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 该论文指出，常用的三数汇总（最小值、中位数、最大值）所含信息不足以可靠估计标准差，可能导致严重偏差；作者通过敏感性分析评估风险，并引入基于缩放Beta分布的新估计量，同时提供Web应用工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在荟萃分析中，部分研究报告均值和标准差，而另一些仅报告分位数汇总（三数或五数汇总）。目前将汇总统计量转换为均值和标准差的方法虽有所发展，但作者发现最常用的三数汇总（最小值、中位数、最大值）本质上缺乏足够信息来可靠推断标准差，可能导致不可靠的估计，进而使推断失效。因此，需要系统评估这一风险并提供改进方案。

Method: 作者首先论证三数汇总信息不足导致SD估计不可靠；其次，提出敏感性分析框架，帮助研究者评估结果的稳健性并识别潜在偏倚来源；此外，探索通过引入额外信息（如对数据分布形状的先验知识）来改善SD估计，并提出基于缩放Beta分布的新估计量；最后，通过模拟研究和真实数据实例验证方法优劣，并开发了在线Web应用以支持敏感性分析。

Result: 模拟与真实数据实例表明：仅依赖三数汇总的SD估计可能产生极差的估计结果，足以使荟萃分析推断失效；引入额外形状信息并使用缩放Beta分布估计量可在一定程度上改善SD估计精度；敏感性分析能有效揭示估计的可靠性范围及偏倚来源。

Conclusion: 三数汇总本质上不足以可靠估计标准差，研究者应对此保持警惕；建议在荟萃分析中使用敏感性分析评估潜在偏倚；当可获取额外分布形状信息时，基于缩放Beta分布的新估计量可作为一种改进方案；配套Web应用可辅助研究者实施上述分析。

Abstract: In recent years, there has been much progress toward the development of methods for converting three- and five-number summary statistics (i.e. minimum, maximum, median, and quartiles) to means and standard deviations (SDs). This is commonly done in the meta-analysis setting, where some studies report means and SDs, while other report quantile summaries. However, we show that three-number summaries, which are the most common, do not contain enough information to reliably estimate SDs. We show that very poor estimates can result, which may invalidate any inference and provide details of a sensitivity analysis that can allow researchers to have greater confidence in their results, or highlight potential sources of bias. We further explore whether nominating additional information can provide enough information regarding the unknown data shape to improve SD estimations, and in doing so introduce a new estimator using the scaled Beta distribution. Simulations and a real data example are used to highlight the advantages and disadvantages of this approach. A Web application is also provided to help researchers perform sensitivity analyses.

</details>


### [149] [Improving Linear Regression on Small Datasets via Gaussian Process and Extreme Value Theory-Based Data Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17486)
*Ibrahim Salay,Jagath Senarathne*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 针对小样本回归分析中经典假设（正态性、同方差性、残差独立性）常被违反的问题，本文提出GP-MEVT方法——一种融合高斯过程与极值理论的新型混合数据增强方法，能扩展预测变量空间并保持线性结构，在模拟和真实数据中均显著优于标准自助法和加噪自助法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小样本条件下回归分析面临严重挑战：经典假设（正态性、同方差性、残差独立性）频繁被违反，导致参数估计精度下降、统计功效降低、结论可推广性受限。现有方法难以有效解决这一问题，需要一种能够在保留原有数据结构的同时合理扩展样本空间的数据增强手段。

Method: 提出GP-MEVT（Gaussian Process-based Modified Extreme Value Theorem）方法：将高斯过程（GP）与修正极值定理（MEVT）相结合。利用GP对残差结构建模，通过极值理论在观测范围之外生成增强样本点，既保持了原始线性结构，又基于残差变异引入受控的随机性。通过三种方差场景（σ=2,5,8）和三种样本量（n=10,15,20）的全面模拟研究进行验证，并与标准自助法和加噪自助法对比。最终在真实数据集上进行了应用验证。

Result: （1）假设满足率：GP-MEVT显著优于标准自助法和加噪自助法；（2）参数估计精度：截距和斜率估计值更接近真实参数；（3）模型拟合：RMSE指标上保持竞争性或更优表现；（4）真实数据验证：GP-MEVT假设满足率达67.1%，远高于自助法的17.3%和21.2%。

Conclusion: GP-MEVT为小样本线性回归建模提供了一个稳健可靠的框架，在样本量受限不可避免的情况下，为研究者提供了一种有原则的统计推断方法。

Abstract: Small sample sizes pose significant challenges in regression analysis, often leading to violations of classical assumptions such as normality, homoscedasticity, and independence of residuals. These violations compromise parameter estimation accuracy, reduce statistical power, and limit the generalizability of findings. This study introduces the Gaussian Process-based Modified Extreme Value Theorem (GP-MEVT) method, a novel hybrid data augmentation approach that combines Gaussian Process with Extreme Value Theory to address these limitations. The GP-MEVT method generates augmented observations that extend the predictor space beyond the observed range while preserving the underlying linear structure and introducing controlled variability based on residual variation, through comprehensive simulation studies across three variance scenarios (sigma = 2, 5, 8) and sample sizes (n = 10, 15, 20). Here, we demonstrate that GP-MEVT achieves a higher rate of assumption satisfaction, substantially outperforming standard bootstrap and bootstrap with noise methods. The proposed method also exhibits reasonable parameter estimation accuracy, with intercept and slope estimates consistently closer to true parameter values, and maintains competitive or superior model fitting performance as measured by root mean square error. Application to a real-world dataset confirms these advantages, with GP-MEVT achieving a 67.1% assumption satisfaction rate compared to 17.3% and 21.2% for bootstrap alternatives. These findings establish GP-MEVT as a robust and reliable framework for fitting linear regression models to small datasets, offering practitioners a principled approach to statistical inference when sample size limitations are unavoidable.

</details>


### [150] [Anytime-valid Optimal Policy Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17515)
*Daniel Molitor*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从日志上下文赌博机数据中进行最优策略识别的随时有效（anytime-valid）框架。该方法构造了随时间索引的置信集，能在任意停止时间以高概率捕获真实最优策略，允许分析人员持续监测证据并在数据依赖的时间点停止，同时保持统计推断有效性。理论分析给出了唯一最优策略下的样本复杂度界，仿真和应用验证了方法相对于固定样本设计的显著样本节省效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际应用中，分析人员需要从外部确定的日志策略产生的数据中识别最优策略，且希望持续监测证据、在证据充分时提前停止，而非预先固定样本量。传统方法无法支持这种可选的连续监测和提前停止，因此需要一种随时有效的统计推断框架。

Method: 基于上下文赌博机模型，构造一个随时间索引的置信集 S_t，利用随时有效（anytime-valid）的集中不等式保证该置信集在所有时间点上以高概率一致覆盖真实最优策略集。分析人员可据此实时剔除明显次优策略。当最优策略唯一时，定义识别该策略的停止时间，并基于最优与次优策略值之间的gap（Δ_min）推导样本复杂度上界。

Result: 理论上，当最优策略唯一时，停止时间的样本复杂度上界为 O((log|Π| + log log(1/Δ_min)) / Δ_min²)。仿真实验表明，该方法相比固定样本量设计能实现显著的样本节省。在减少在线错误信息的大型自适应实验应用中，该方法提供了证据积累的动态视图。

Conclusion: 所提出的随时有效框架能够灵活支持从日志赌博机数据中在线识别最优策略，在保持统计有效性的同时实现显著的数据效率提升，适用于需要实时决策和动态监测的应用场景。

Abstract: We develop an anytime-valid framework for optimal policy identification from logged contextual bandit data. In many applied settings, the analyst wants to select the optimal policy from a candidate policy class $Π$, but data are generated by an externally determined logging policy that they do not control. The analyst may also wish to monitor evidence continuously, stopping once the optimal policy is clear rather than committing to a fixed sample size in advance. This paper addresses these challenges by constructing a time-indexed set $S_t$ that retains the true optimal policy set uniformly over time with high probability. The resulting procedure allows the analyst to monitor policy values, eliminate clearly suboptimal policies, and stop at data-dependent times without invalidating inference. When the optimal policy is unique, we define a stopping time for its identification and derive a sample-complexity bound scaling as $O\!\left(\frac{\log |Π|+\log\log(1/Δ_{\min})}{Δ_{\min}^2}\right)$, where $Δ_{\min}$ is the gap between the best and second-best policy values. Simulations demonstrate that the anytime-valid approach can yield substantial sample savings relative to fixed-$N$ designs. An application to a large adaptive experiment on reducing misinformation online illustrates how the method provides a dynamic view as evidence on the optimal policy accumulates.

</details>


### [151] [Proximal Mediation Analysis with Hidden Recanting Witnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17600)
*Sihan Wu,Yang Bai,Yifan Cui*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文针对存在未知"翻供证人"（recanting witnesses，即受治疗诱导的中介-结果混杂因子）的路径特异性效应识别难题，利用近端因果推断提出三种新型识别策略，并构建了半参数推断框架，包括高效影响函数、近端多重稳健估计量及基于极小极大优化的去偏机器学习流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统中介分析在分解直接和间接因果路径时，通常假设不存在或完全已知"翻供证人"——即受治疗诱导的中介-结果混杂因子。然而在实际中，这些变量常因测量困难或隐私约束而不可观测，使得该严格假设难以满足。作者旨在突破这一限制，在翻供证人未知的情形下实现路径特异性效应的识别与推断。

Method: （1）利用近端因果推断框架，提出三种新颖的识别策略，在未知翻供证人存在下识别路径特异性效应。（2）构建半参数推断框架，推导高效影响函数（EIF），提出近端多重稳健估计量：只要至少一组冗余模型被正确指定，估计量即保持一致性；当所有模型均正确指定且以适当速率收敛时，可达半参数效率界。（3）采用基于极小极大优化的去偏机器学习流程进行点估计并构建有效置信区间。

Result: 近端多重稳健估计量在至少一组冗余模型正确指定时具有一致性；当所有冗余模型正确指定且收敛速率满足条件时，估计量渐近正态并达到半参数效率界。模拟研究和真实数据应用均验证了所提方法的优良性能。

Conclusion: 本文成功将近端因果推断引入中介分析，解决了未知翻供证人下的路径特异性效应识别难题，所提出的半参数推断框架和近端多重稳健估计量在理论和实践上均表现出色，为实际应用中的因果路径分解提供了可靠工具。

Abstract: Mediation analysis is essential for decomposing the causal effect of a treatment into direct and indirect pathways. However, many practical settings rely on the stringent assumption that recanting witnesses, defined as treatment-induced mediator-outcome confounders, are either absent or fully known a priori. Such a requirement is often untenable, especially when these variables remain unobservable due to measurement difficulties or privacy constraints. In this paper, we leverage proximal causal inference to develop three novel identification strategies to address the challenge of identifying path-specific effects in the presence of unknown recanting witnesses. Building on this, we develop a semiparametric inference framework that derives the efficient influence function and proposes a proximal multiply robust estimator, which remains consistent if at least one set of nuisance models is correctly specified. When all nuisance models are correctly specified and converge at appropriate rates, the estimator is asymptotically normal and achieves the semiparametric efficiency bound. We provide a minimax optimization-based debiased machine learning procedure for point estimation and constructing valid confidence intervals. The performance of the proposed methods is demonstrated by simulation studies and a real data application.

</details>


### [152] [Double zero-inflated spatio-temporal modeling of daily precipitation under detection thresholds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17717)
*Juan Marcen-Gutierrez,Jorge Castillo-Mateo,Alan E. Gelfand,Jesús Asín,Ana C. Cebrián*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出一种多层时空模型，区分并解释两类零值（真实干事件与检测限导致的零值），采用Probit回归、Gamma回归与阈值截尾观测机制，结合高斯过程捕捉空间依赖，在贝叶斯框架下对西班牙埃布罗河流域春季降水进行应用分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日尺度降水行为建模面临大量零值的挑战，且零值存在两种不同来源：真实无降水的干事件，以及因检测限限制未能观测到的小量降水。传统方法往往混淆二者，导致对降水机制的误判，因此需要能够区分并解释两类零值的精细建模方法。

Method: 构建三层结构的时空模型：(1) 通过Probit回归对零值概率建模，采用点质量混合分布；(2) 对超过检测限的潜在正降水采用Gamma回归；(3) 引入阈值截尾的观测机制。各回归层均嵌入高斯过程以捕捉空间依赖性。模型在贝叶斯框架下进行推断，可获得精确的不确定性量化。

Result: 在西班牙埃布罗河流域70个站点15年春季日降水数据的应用中，发现检测阈值对观测降水发生概率有显著影响，尤其在湿润地区；该阈值对总累积降水量的影响较小，但对高尾分位数存在不可忽视的影响。

Conclusion: 所提模型能够有效区分两类零值，揭示检测限在不同区域和不同统计特征上的差异化影响，为日尺度降水机制研究提供了更精细的推断工具。

Abstract: Explaining precipitation behavior at daily scale is important for fine scale understanding of the mechanisms driving precipitation. However, this effort is challenging because of the frequent incidence of zeros. The challenge is amplified by the acknowledged incidence of two types of zeros -- absence of precipitation as a dry event and absence of measured precipitation due to detection limits. In this work, we propose a multilevel spatio-temporal model which allows us to distinguish and explain the two types of zeros, as well as to model positive precipitation above the detection limit. The methodology combines a point mass at zero with probability modeled through a probit regression, a Gamma regression for latent positive precipitation amounts, and an observation mechanism subject to threshold-induced censoring. To capture spatial dependencies, Gaussian processes are employed in each regression model. Working within a Bayesian framework, we can obtain a rich range of inference with exact uncertainty. In particular, we provide model-based inference tools to compare and quantify differences between the true precipitation process and its observed counterpart across relevant characteristics. We apply our model to the analysis of daily spring observations at 70 sites over 15 years from the Ebro River Basin in northeastern Spain. Our findings indicate that the threshold strongly affects the occurrence of observed precipitation, especially in humid regions. While its impact on total accumulated amounts is small, it can exert a relevant effect on upper quantiles.

</details>


### [153] [On Response-Adaptive Targeting Strategies for Multi-Treatment Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17777)
*Redouane Yagouti,Rémy Degenne,Emilie Kaufmann*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了α-重平衡靶向策略（αRTS），一个用于多臂临床试验响应适应性随机化的统一框架，将两臂ERADE策略推广至K≥2臂情形。该框架下的所有设计均具备强相合性、分配比例和治疗效应估计的渐近正态性及渐近效率。针对稀疏靶向分配问题，作者进一步提出带强制探索的αRTS变体，确保所有治疗组无限采样并保持渐近保证。三臂模拟验证了有限样本表现，尤其凸显强制探索在稀疏场景中的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多臂（K≥2）响应适应性随机化（RAR）在理论层面仍较为零散，现有方法多聚焦于特定算法或受限的靶向分配，缺乏统一的渐近理论框架。作者旨在弥合这一空白，将两臂ERADE策略推广至一般多臂情形，建立统一的渐近性质分析体系，并解决稀疏靶向分配下部分治疗组可能被渐近剔除的问题。

Method: 作者提出α-重平衡靶向策略（αRTS）统一框架，以参数α∈[0,1]控制随机化程度与靶向分配之间的权衡。框架涵盖多种已有设计（如ERADE、DBCD等）作为特例。理论部分利用鞅方法和随机逼近理论，统一证明了所有αRTS设计的强相合性、分配比例的渐近正态性以及治疗效应估计量的渐近正态性与渐近效率。针对稀疏靶向分配（某些治疗组最优分配比例趋于零），提出带强制探索的αRTS变体，通过引入正下界采样概率保证所有处理组均被无限次采样，同时维持原有渐近性质。

Result: 理论上，统一证明了αRTS族所有设计均满足：分配比例几乎必然收敛至目标最优分配；标准化分配比例及治疗效应估计量均服从渐近正态分布；治疗效应估计量达到渐近方差下界（渐近效率）。模拟研究在三臂试验设置下对比多种αRTS变体的有限样本表现，结果表明：在非稀疏场景下各变体表现良好；在稀疏靶向场景中，无强制探索的αRTS可能导致某些治疗组样本量不足，而带强制探索的变体能有效维持所有治疗组的充分采样，且估计精度与检验功效均显著改善。

Conclusion: αRTS框架为多臂RAR提供了统一的理论基础，证明了该族设计的强相合性、渐近正态性和渐近效率。强制探索机制是处理稀疏靶向分配的关键补救手段，在维持渐近最优性的同时确保实际试验中所有治疗组均获得充分信息。该框架为多臂适应性试验设计提供了灵活且理论完备的工具箱。

Abstract: Response-adaptive randomization (RAR) in clinical trials aims to improve ethical and statistical efficiency by dynamically allocating patients to treatments based on observed outcomes. While RAR based on a target optimal allocation have been extensively studied for two-arms settings, their extension to multi-treatment experiments ($K \geq 2$) remains theoretically fragmented, with most existing methods focusing on specific algorithms or restricted target allocations. In this paper, we introduce a unified framework for response-adaptive targeting, the $α$-Rebalancing Targeting Strategies ($α$RTS), which generalize the ERADE two-armed strategy of Hu et al. [2009]. We prove that all designs in this family share fundamental asymptotic properties: strong consistency, asymptotic normality of allocation proportions and treatment effect estimators, and asymptotic efficiency. To address sparse target regimes (where some treatments are asymptotically eliminated), we further propose $α$RTS with Forced Exploration, a variant that guarantees infinite sampling for all treatments while preserving the asymptotic guarantees. Extensive simulations illustrate the finite-sample behavior of $α$RTS variants in a 3-armed context, highlighting in particular the critical role of forced exploration in sparse settings.

</details>


### [154] [Subgroup analysis in randomized controlled trials with binary outcomes: dilution and logic-respecting properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17841)
*Long-Hao Xu,Yang Han,Tim Friede*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文从理论上证明了比值比(odds ratio)在亚组分析中通常不满足"逻辑一致性"和存在"稀释效应"，而相对响应(relative response)是合适的疗效指标；同时阐明了二者的形式化关系及其近似一致的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机对照试验中，亚组分析用于考察疗效是否在各患者亚组间一致。比值比虽被广泛使用，但其在合并亚组时会出现违反直觉的现象（如总体效应不在亚组效应之间、向1稀释），因此需要从理论上系统阐明比值比不适用于亚组分析的根源，并指出合适的替代指标。

Method: 本文采用理论推导的方法，建立若干新定理，刻画当两个亚组合并后总体比值比在大小和方向上的变化规律；同时给出比值比与相对响应的形式化关系，并定义"逻辑一致性"（logic-respecting property）和"稀释性"（dilution property）两个概念框架来比较两种指标的性质。

Result: 定理表明：比值比合并亚组时通常不满足逻辑一致性（总体效应不在亚组效应之间），且存在向1稀释的趋势；相对响应则满足逻辑一致性且无稀释问题。不过在特定条件下，比值比可近似表现为逻辑一致。临床数据示例进一步验证了理论结论。

Conclusion: 比值比不适合作为亚组分析中的疗效指标，相对响应更为合适。研究者应审慎选择亚组分析中的效应量，避免因比值比的反直觉行为导致误导性结论。

Abstract: Subgroup analysis is routinely used in randomized controlled trials to examine whether treatment effects are homogeneous across patient subgroups or differ because of treatment-effect heterogeneity. In this paper, we investigate the properties of the odds ratio and the relative response in subgroup analyses with binary outcomes, extending previous work with new theoretical insights and methodological developments. We establish several new theorems that characterize how the odds ratio for the overall population changes in both magnitude and direction when two subgroups are combined. These results further confirm that the odds ratio is inappropriate as an efficacy measure in this subgroup setting, whereas the relative response is appropriate. We also present the formal relationship between the odds ratio and the relative response, and clarify their differences in terms of the logic-respecting property, that is, whether the overall efficacy lies between the subgroup efficacies, and the dilution property, that is, whether mixing subgroups moves the overall odds ratio toward 1. Although the odds ratio is generally not logic-respecting, it may behave approximately like a logic-respecting efficacy measure under certain conditions. To illustrate our findings, we present an illustrative example based on clinical trial data and discuss its implications for subgroup analysis in randomized controlled trials.

</details>


### [155] [Spatial mixed models for assessing environmental exposure effects on the microbiome](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17923)
*Sooran Kim,Chan Wang,Soyoung Kwak,Fares Darawshy,Alexander Bain,Leopoldo N. Segal,Jiyoung Ahn,Huilin Li*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于微生物组数据的空间混合建模框架，通过条件自回归先验同时考虑区域空间依赖和微生物类群间的生态相关性，在模拟和真实空气污染（PM2.5）研究中展示出优于现有方法的特征选择检测功效和估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环境暴露（如空气污染）对宿主健康的影响可能由微生物组介导，但当前统计建模方法忽视了两类关键依赖结构——采样区域间的空间依赖和微生物类群间的生态相关性——导致检测效能显著降低、遗漏真实信号。

Method: 提出一种新型空间混合建模框架，利用条件自回归（conditional autoregressive, CAR）先验同时建模区域层面的空间依赖和类群层面的生态依赖，实现高维微生物组数据的特征选择与效应估计。

Result: 模拟研究表明，该框架在特征选择中检测功效高、假阳性率低、均方误差减小。在两个真实数据集（FAMiLI研究和肺部微生物组数据集）中应用PM2.5暴露数据，成功识别出已知与污染相关健康结局有关的菌属以及可能介导宿主对空气污染响应的新类群。

Conclusion: 该空间混合建模方法为复杂环境数据中揭示具有生物学意义的微生物组-环境关联提供了强大而灵活的工具，能够有效克服传统方法因忽视空间和生态依赖而导致的统计效能不足问题。

Abstract: The influence of environmental exposures, such as air pollution, on human health has become increasingly recognized. A growing body of evidence suggests that the microbiome may mediate these effects, explaining the relationship between the environment and host biology. However, the impact of environmental exposures on the microbiome is not yet fully understood, and statistical modeling in this context is challenged by complex dependency structures. In particular, microbiome data exhibit spatial dependencies across sampling regions as well as ecological correlations among microbial taxa, which, if ignored, can substantially reduce detection power, leading to missed true signals. We introduce a novel spatial mixed modeling framework for microbiome data that accounts for both region-level spatial dependency and taxon-level ecological dependency using conditional autoregressive priors. Through simulations, we demonstrate that this framework outperforms existing methods that ignore such dependencies, by achieving high detection power in feature selection while maintaining low false positive rates and reduced mean squared error in estimation. Applied to two real studies-data from Food and Microbiome Longitudinal Investigation study and lung microbiome dataset-with fine particulate matter (PM_2.5) exposures, our model identified genera, which are known to be involved in pollution-related health outcomes, as well as novel taxa that may mediate host responses to air pollution. This novel approach offers a powerful and flexible tool for uncovering biologically meaningful associations in complex environmental data.

</details>


### [156] [Spatial prediction of environmental processes using random forests: How best to account for spatial dependence?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18078)
*Duncan Lee,Vinny Davies,Helen R. Savage,Hussein Twabi,Marriott Nliwasa,Peter MacPherson*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了将随机森林与空间自相关建模相融合的多种方法，通过模拟实验和马拉维空气污染实测数据发现，基于空间基函数的方法在预测性能上表现最为稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地统计空间预测通常依赖高斯过程/克里金方法，而机器学习在非空间预测中表现优异。近年涌现出多种融合ML与空间自相关的混合方法，但缺乏对其相对预测性能的系统数值比较，环境科学家无从选择最优方案。

Method: 以随机森林为基础ML算法，比较四类融合策略：(1) 与高斯过程融合；(2) 观测驱动的相关结构；(3) 空间基函数；(4) 局部地理拟合。通过两项研究评估：一是涵盖不同空间自相关类型的受控模拟实验，二是马拉维布兰太尔市结核病流行研究中的空气污染浓度预测实测数据。

Result: 模拟与实测结果均表明，不存在在所有场景下普遍最优的单一方法；但基于空间基函数的方法在两类研究中均表现出一致且良好的预测性能。

Conclusion: 虽然无方法能在所有情况下绝对胜出，但空间基函数方法具有最稳定的跨场景表现，可推荐给环境科学实践者优先考虑。该研究填补了空间机器学习方法系统比较的空白。

Abstract: Geostatistical spatial prediction for environmental processes is typically undertaken using Gaussian process models via Kriging, while machine learning (ML) algorithms are state-of-the-art for non-spatial prediction. An exciting recent fusion of these ideas imbibes traditional ML algorithms with the capacity to deal with spatial autocorrelation, leading to improved predictive performance. A range of approaches have been proposed, including fusion with Gaussian processes, observation-driven correlation structures, spatial basis functions and local geographical fitting. However, there has been no numerical comparison of their relative predictive performances, which is needed to advise environmental scientists on the optimal approach to use. This paper fills this knowledge gap, and focuses on random forests as the ML algorithm because they are more computationally and conceptually straightforward to implement than deep learning algorithms. The results from two studies are presented, the first being a controlled simulation experiment investigating whether any single approach is consistently superior across different spatial autocorrelation types. The second study focuses on the prediction of air pollution concentrations within a tuberculosis prevalence study in Blantyre, Malawi. The results show that whilst no single approach is universally superior, utilising spatial basis functions appears to perform consistently well across both the simulation and real data studies.

</details>


### [157] [Undocumented Behavior in the gsynth R package and its Consequences for Three Published Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18113)
*Beniamino Green,P. M. Aronow*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: CRAN上gsynth包在1.3.1版本之前存在一个实现错误：当同时使用parametric bootstrap和EM算法估计IFE模型时，错误地使用了样本内残差而非样本外误差，导致标准误被系统性地严重低估，进而产生高假阳性率。该错误已影响三篇发表在APSR上的论文，修正后结果大多不再显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: gsynth是估计交互固定效应（IFE）模型的流行R包，但作者发现其在特定设置下会系统性地严重低估标准误，这可能导致实证研究中出现大量虚假显著结果。作者旨在识别并量化该实现错误的严重程度，同时评估其对已发表政治科学研究的实际影响。

Method: 作者识别出gsynth包在1.3.1版本前的一个具体实现错误：当EM=TRUE且inference="parametric"时，参数化自助法使用了样本内残差而非Xu (2017)所提出的样本外误差。为评估其影响，作者采用基于随机分配的安慰剂处理对多个州级面板数据集进行了实证蒙特卡洛模拟，并重新分析了三篇受影响的APSR论文。

Result: 实证蒙特卡洛模拟表明，该实现错误可导致标准误被低估数个数量级，并在现实设定中产生高假阳性率。三篇APSR论文的重新分析显示：(i) 修正实现错误后，大多数发现变为不显著；(ii) 使用Xu (2017)的广义合成控制法替代IFE-EM后，所有发现均不显著。

Conclusion: gsynth包1.3.1之前版本的实现错误具有严重性，不仅理论上与Gobillon和Magnac (2016)的IFE-EM估计量不兼容，而且在实际应用中可能导致错误的统计推断。该研究强调了计量软件实现准确性的重要性，并警示使用该包早期版本的研究者需重新审视其结果。

Abstract: Prior to the version 1.3.1 update on CRAN in December 2025, gsynth, a popular R package for estimating Interactive Fixed Effects (IFE) models, could drastically and systematically underestimate standard errors. This underestimation would occur when two estimation options (inference = "parametric", and EM = TRUE) were used together, in which case the package would apply a parametric bootstrap procedure to Gobillon and Magnac (2016)'s IFE-EM estimator. The package ceased supporting this combination in December 2025, and the latest documentation now describes the parametric bootstrap as not suitable for use with the IFE-EM estimator due to a theoretical incompatibility. Our focus is an implementation error we identified in the pre-1.3.1 versions of gsynth: the parametric bootstrap used when EM = TRUE did not match the algorithm proposed in Xu (2017), using in-sample residuals instead of out-of-sample errors. We show that this implementation error alone can cause underestimation by orders of magnitude. We conduct an empirical Monte Carlo study using randomly assigned placebo treatments on a series of state-level panel datasets, and show that gsynth could yield high false positive rates in realistic settings. We identify three papers published in the American Political Science Review that are affected by this behavior. Reanalyzing the relevant sections of these papers, we show that (i) correcting the implementation error renders most findings insignificant, and (ii) using Xu (2017)'s Generalized Synthetic Control method in place of IFE-EM renders every finding insignificant.

</details>


### [158] [Bayesian Threshold-Aligned Joint Disease Progression Modeling for Alzheimer's Disease](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18139)
*Rong Wu,Duygu Tosun,Isabella Hausle,Margo Heston,Aaron Wolfe Scheffler*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: B-TAJ DPM 是一个贝叶斯半参数联合模型，通过在由标志物阳性阈值锚定的潜 time 线上联合建模多变量疾病进展轨迹与认知障碍生存终点，克服了现有模型的参数限制和解耦缺陷，在ADNI数据中揭示了tau阳性年龄与认知衰退加速之间的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿尔茨海默病的病理进展与认知症状出现的时间在个体间存在巨大异质性，但现有神经退行性疾病进展模型存在三个关键局限：(1) 依赖限制性参数形式；(2) 未能将疾病时间线锚定到生物标志物的阳性阈值；(3) 将生物标志物轨迹与认知生存终点解耦处理。因此需要一种能联合建模疾病进展与认知损害时间、并以阳性阈值为锚点对齐个体时间线的统一框架。

Method: 提出 B-TAJ DPM（Bayesian Threshold-Aligned Joint Disease Progression Model），这是一个生成式半参数贝叶斯框架。核心设计包括：(1) 在潜疾病时间线上建模多变量疾病进展轨迹；(2) 以标志物阳性阈值作为时间锚点对齐不同个体；(3) 集成生存模型将病理进展与认知障碍发生时间关联。模型通过开源软件实现后验推断和对未见个体的后验预测。

Result: 模拟研究显示该模型具有优异的估计精度和区间覆盖率。在ADNI数据应用中，B-TAJ DPM成功刻画了非线性进展模式，量化了阳性年龄的个体间差异，并揭示了tau蛋白阳性年龄与认知障碍加速之间的显著关联。

Conclusion: B-TAJ DPM 突破了现有疾病进展模型的参数和结构限制，通过阈值锚定和联合生存建模，为理解阿尔茨海默病异质性的来源——特别是tau病理时机对认知衰退的影响——提供了强有力的统计工具。

Abstract: Alzheimer's disease is characterized by the progressive accumulation of amyloid-$β$ and tau followed years later by cognitive impairment. Despite this established motif, substantial subject-level variability exists in the age of pathological progression and the onset of cognitive symptoms. To understand the source of this variation, subjects must be aligned across heterogeneous disease timelines via frameworks that jointly model disease progression and time to cognitive impairment with reference to landmark positivity thresholds. Existing neurodegenerative disease progression models rely on restrictive parametric forms, fail to anchor disease timelines to positivity thresholds, and decouple biomarker trajectories from cognitive survival endpoints. To address these limitations, we introduce the Bayesian Threshold-Aligned Joint Disease Progression Model (B-TAJ DPM). This generative, semi-parametric framework models multivariate disease progression trajectories over latent disease timelines anchored at landmark positivity thresholds. Crucially, the framework integrates a survival model to link pathological progression to cognitive impairment. Posterior inference and posterior predictions for unseen subjects are carried out in open-source software. Simulation studies demonstrate excellent estimation accuracy and interval coverage. When applied to Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative data, B-TAJ DPM characterizes non-linear progression patterns, quantifies subject-level variation in positivity age, and reveals links between age of tau positivity and acceleration of cognitive impairment.

</details>


### [159] [Spatial Disease Mapping and Disparity Detection Using Generative AI: An Amortized Bayesian Learning Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18146)
*Luca Aiello,Sudipto Banerjee*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向空间边界检测的摊销贝叶斯框架，通过神经网络引擎将后验推断泛化至不同区域数量和邻接结构的区域图上。框架耦合Poisson计数似然与协变量驱动的平滑中断规则，并利用DAGAR先验捕捉残差空间依赖性。仿真和实证表明，该方法在恢复参数、区间覆盖、后验预测及边界概率推断方面表现良好，且可在不同真实地图间无缝部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统空间边界检测依赖MCMC逐图重新拟合，计算代价高昂且无法跨图泛化。作者旨在构建一个摊销推断框架，使单一训练好的神经网络能够在新区域图（不同区域数、不同邻接结构）上即时生成后验边界概率，从而大幅提升计算效率与部署灵活性。

Method: 底层模型：Poisson计数似然 + 协变量驱动的Wombling边界检测（相邻区域协变量差异大时中断平滑） + DAGAR先验捕捉残差空间自相关。推断框架：训练阶段在模拟地图上学习——(1) 置换不变汇总网络将观测计数、偏移量、协变量及邻接矩阵编码为图感知表示；(2) 条件标准化流从该表示生成近似后验抽取。部署阶段直接用训练好的引擎在新图上进行摊销推断，无需重新拟合。

Result: 仿真研究表明：(1) 参数恢复准确；(2) 区间覆盖接近名义水平；(3) 后验预测行为校准良好；(4) 后验边界概率具有信息性。与MCMC基准对比，边界证据高度一致。消融实验验证了模型引导的图汇总信息的必要性。在Glasgow呼吸系统疾病数据和California肺癌数据上的应用表明，单一训练引擎可在不同图结构上无缝部署，边界结论与既有局部平滑分析一致。

Conclusion: 摊销贝叶斯推断可有效解决空间边界检测中跨图泛化的难题，单一神经网络引擎在模拟和真实数据上均表现出与传统MCMC相当的推断质量，同时具备即时部署的显著计算优势。

Abstract: We introduce an amortized Bayesian framework for spatial boundary detection that generalizes posterior inference across areal graphs with varying numbers of regions and diverse adjacency structures. The underlying model couples a Poisson count likelihood with a covariate-driven rule to interrupt smoothing across dissimilar neighboring areas, utilizing a directed acyclic graph autoregressive (DAGAR) prior to capture residual spatial dependence. To approximate the target posterior distribution, a neural engine is trained on simulated maps: a permutation-invariant summary network encodes graph-aware representations of the observed counts, offsets, covariates, and adjacency matrices, while a conditional normalizing flow generates the approximate posterior draws. Simulation studies demonstrate accurate parameter recovery, near-nominal interval coverage, well-calibrated posterior predictive behavior, and informative posterior boundary probabilities. Benchmarking against Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) confirms close agreement regarding primary boundary evidence, and an ablation study validates the inclusion of model-guided graph summaries. Finally, applications to Glasgow respiratory disease and California lung cancer data demonstrate that a single trained neural engine can be seamlessly deployed across real-world maps with distinct graph structures, yielding boundary conclusions consistent with established localized smoothing analyses.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [160] [Another Look at Log-PCA for Probability Measures: A Dynamical Formulation and Statistical Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17196)
*Peng Xu,Changbo Zhu,Young-Heon Kim,Xiaohui Chen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出Wasserstein切向PCA（WT-PCA），通过动态变分形式重新解释对数PCA，利用协方差算子捕捉Wasserstein空间中概率测度的局部主测地线变异模式，并基于最优传输的平行传输结构给出了经验估计的统计收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Wasserstein几何下随机概率测度的主变异分析，旨在为Wasserstein空间中的概率测度提供一种可微分的、局部的主测地线分析方法，克服传统对数PCA的局限性。

Method: 1. 引入新的动力学公式，将对数PCA重新解释为变分方法；2. 提出WT-PCA，利用重心处的协方差算子捕捉加权概率测度在Wasserstein空间中的局部主测地线变异模式；3. 基于最优传输问题的平行传输结构，推导经验WT-PCA的统计收敛率。

Result: 推导出经验WT-PCA在以2-Wasserstein距离度量下，总体与经验重心参考测度之间的统计收敛率。

Conclusion: WT-PCA为Wasserstein空间中的概率测度主变异分析提供了一种理论上可保证收敛的、可微分的局部PCA方法，丰富了Wasserstein几何下的统计学习理论。

Abstract: This paper is concerned with learning principal variations of random probability measures on $\mathbb{R}^m$ under the Wasserstein geometry. We introduce a new dynamical formulation to interpret the log-PCA, a linearized principal geodesic analysis, as a variational approach. Our differentiable version, termed as the Wasserstein Tangential PCA (WT-PCA), captures the local principal modes of geodesic variations of a (weighted) probability measure on the Wasserstein space via its covariance operator at barycenter. Based on the dynamical perspective and leveraging parallel transport structure of the optimal transport problems, we derive a general statistical convergence rate of the empirical WT-PCA when estimated from data in terms of the 2-Wasserstein distance between the population and empirical barycenter reference measures.

</details>


### [161] [Tight $L_\infty$ Sample Complexity for Low-Degree and Sparse Boolean Polynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17319)
*Jasper van Doornmalen,Mathieu Molina,Victor Verdugo,José Verschae*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究在布尔超立方体上学习多项式代理的样本复杂度，目标是在 $L_\infty$ 误差保证下实现优化安全。对于 $n$ 变量、度数至多 $d$ 的多项式，样本复杂度为 $n^{d+1}$；对于 $s$-稀疏 Fourier-Walsh 多项式，样本复杂度为 $ns^2$。这些下界即使在自适应学习器下也成立，结果与无噪声情形存在结构性差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者受有界二进制黑箱函数优化问题的驱动，指出传统的 $L_2$ 型误差保证无法确保代理模型的最优解对原目标函数也是好的解；需要 $L_\infty$（一致）误差保证来保障优化安全性。

Method: 针对两类有界多项式——度数受限多项式与稀疏 Fourier-Walsh 多项式——分别在亚高斯噪声下，利用辅助范数作为控制 $L_\infty$ 误差的代理，推导均匀估计的极小极大样本复杂度上界与下界。下界对任意自适应学习器成立。

Result: （1）度数 $\leq d$ 的多项式：样本复杂度 $\Theta(n^{d+1})$；（2）$s$-稀疏 Fourier-Walsh 多项式（$s \leq n$）：样本复杂度 $\Theta(ns^2)$。无噪声情形下精确恢复分别为 $n^d$ 和 $ns$，噪声引入的额外因子是内在的。

Conclusion: 本文给出了学习优化安全多项式代理的样本复杂度的紧致刻画，揭示了 $L_\infty$ 一致估计与 $L_2$ 估计在样本需求上的结构性差异，证明了噪声环境下额外因子的不可避免性。

Abstract: Motivated by the optimization of bounded binary black-box functions, we study the problem of learning polynomial surrogates over the Boolean hypercube. To ensure that optimizing the surrogate yields good solutions for the underlying objective, we require uniform $L_\infty$-error guarantees rather than the usual $L_2$-type guarantees. We characterize the minimax sample complexity of uniform estimation under subgaussian noise for two classes of bounded polynomials. First, for polynomials of degree at most $d$ on $n$ variables, the sample complexity scales as $n^{d+1}$. Second, for $s$-sparse Fourier-Walsh polynomials with $s \leq n$, it scales as $ns^2$. These rates differ structurally from the noiseless setting, where uniform exact recovery scales as $n^d$ and $ns$, respectively. Our lower bounds hold even for arbitrary adaptive learners, showing that the additional factors are intrinsic to the noisy cases. Standard Fourier-analysis tools for the $L_2$-norm do not naturally extend to the $L_\infty$-setting in a way that yields uniform guarantees. Our proofs overcome this difficulty by relying on suitably chosen auxiliary norms that serve as proxies for controlling the $L_\infty$-error. Together, our results provide a tight characterization of the sample complexity of learning optimization-safe polynomial surrogates.

</details>


### [162] [Bounded Difference Concentration for Infinitely Exchangeable Sequences with Applications to AI Benchmark Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17426)
*Fangyuan Lin,Spencer Frei,Victor H. de la Pena*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了无限可交换随机变量函数的集中性质，通过 de Finetti 测度条件化，将任意有界差分函数的偏差分解为条件抽样波动和潜在混合波动，建立了有效方差代理为 $\frac{1}{4}\sum_i c_i^2 + \sigma_{\mathrm{mix}}^2$ 的集中不等式。对于零和线性对比，潜在混合项精确抵消，得到无混合的 Hoeffding 型界。该框架被应用于 MMLU 等复合 AI 基准测试中的不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可交换随机变量的集中结果主要针对有限可交换情形，且往往受潜在混合效应影响导致界不够紧致。本文旨在通过 de Finetti 表示定理，将无限可交换变量的集中现象分解为可分离的两个波动来源，从而获得更紧致且无需混合项的集中界，并应用于 AI 基准评估中的不确定性量化。

Method: 基于 de Finetti 定理，以 directing measure 为条件将函数偏差分解为"条件抽样波动"和"潜在混合波动"两部分。假设潜在混合为 $\sigma_{\mathrm{mix}}^2$-亚高斯时，建立有效方差代理为两项之和的集中不等式。特别地，对于零和线性对比（如子样本均值与总体均值之差），证明潜在混合项精确抵消，得到紧致的无混合 Hoeffding 型界，并建立与近期有限可交换集中结果的无限可扩展性联系。

Result: （1）建立了无限可交换情形下带有混合项的一般集中不等式，有效方差代理为 $\frac{1}{4}\sum_i c_i^2 + \sigma_{\mathrm{mix}}^2$；（2）证明零和线性对比的混合项精确抵消，得到无混合的 Hoeffding 型界；（3）在 MMLU 等复合 AI 基准测试中，提供了领域分层层次模型以界定准确率分数的不确定性，并给出了通过随机子集估计完整基准分数的分布自由、节约成本的统计保证。

Conclusion: 该研究通过 de Finetti 条件化框架，将无限可交换变量的集中现象清晰地分解为双重波动源，揭示了零和线性对比中混合效应的精确抵消机制，为可交换数据的集中分析提供了理论基础和实用工具，并可有效应用于 AI 基准测试等实际场景的不确定性量化。

Abstract: We consider the concentration properties of functions of infinitely exchangeable random variables. By conditioning on the de Finetti directing measure, we show that the deviation of any function with bounded-difference constants $c_1, \dots, c_n$ decomposes into a conditional sampling fluctuation and a latent mixture fluctuation. When this latent mixture is $σ_{\mathrm{mix}}^2$-subgaussian, we establish a concentration inequality with an effective variance proxy of $\frac{1}{4}\sum_i c_i^2 + σ_{\mathrm{mix}}^2$. Crucially, we demonstrate that for zero-sum linear contrasts, such as the difference between a subsample mean and a full population mean, the latent mixture term cancels exactly. This cancellation yields a tight, mixture-free Hoeffding-type bound that provides a direct de Finetti mechanism for the infinite-extendibility limit of recent finite-exchangeable concentration results. We apply this framework to quantify uncertainty in composite AI benchmarks, such as MMLU, where question items naturally exhibit exchangeable dependence across domains. Our results provide both a domain-stratified hierarchical model for bounding the uncertainty of accuracy scores, and a distribution-free, cost-saving statistical guarantee for accurately estimating full benchmark scores from random subsets.

</details>


### [163] [A Bayesian Boolean Matrix Factorization with Application to Copy Number Analysis in Cancer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17491)
*Adolphus Wagala,Mehmet Samur,Giovanni Parmigiani*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出贝叶斯布尔矩阵分解（BBMF），一种全共轭生成模型，通过稀疏先验和吉布斯采样实现二进制数据的可解释因子分解，并在多发性骨髓瘤染色体臂拷贝数变异数据中验证了其揭示离散潜在结构的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有布尔矩阵分解方法多为启发式、贪心算法，对初始化敏感，易陷入局部最优，且缺乏原则性的模型选择和不确定性量化。癌症基因组学中，肿瘤进化常涉及大规模、近同步的染色体数目变化（如全基因组复制后的不稳定与选择），布尔分解比加性模型更能自然捕捉这些离散共变异模式。

Method: 提出贝叶斯布尔矩阵分解（BBMF）：(1) 构建全共轭生成模型，融入稀疏诱导先验；(2) 强制执行布尔约束，将二进制矩阵分解为两个低秩二进制矩阵的逻辑与/或运算；(3) 推导闭合形式的完全条件分布，实现吉布斯采样进行后验推断，同时获得可解释的潜在因子及一致的 uncertainty quantification。

Result: 在多发性骨髓瘤染色体臂水平拷贝数变异数据上，BBMF 发现了一小组可解释的双团（bicliques）结构，将患者子集与反复共同变异的染色体臂关联起来，提供了肿瘤异质性的紧凑、生物学上有意义的总结。

Conclusion: BBMF 为复杂二进制数据中的离散潜在结构发现提供了一种原则性的贝叶斯方法，兼具可解释性、不确定性量化和模型选择能力，在癌症基因组学等需要捕捉离散共变异模式的应用中展现出显著优势。

Abstract: Binary data factorization is common, but real-valued methods ignore discreteness and yield hard-to-interpret factors. Boolean Matrix Factorization (BooMF) instead decomposes a binary matrix into two lower-rank binary matrices via logical AND and OR, expressing the data as a Boolean disjunction of interpretable patterns. In cancer genomics, BooMF can reveal coordinated feature changes that may drive tumor evolution, unlike rotational or additive decompositions. Most existing BooMF methods are heuristic, greedy, sensitive to initialization, prone to local optima, and do not support principled model selection or uncertainty quantification. We introduce Bayesian Boolean Matrix Factorization (BBMF), a fully conjugate generative model with sparsity-inducing priors. It enforces Boolean constraints, yields interpretable latent factors with coherent uncertainty quantification, and admits Gibbs sampling with closed-form full conditionals. Because cancer evolution often involves widespread, near-simultaneous chromosome-number changes (e.g., whole-genome duplication followed by instability and selection), Boolean factorizations capture these patterns more naturally than additive models. Applied to arm-level copy-number alteration data in multiple myeloma, where entries indicate presence/absence of chromosomal-arm amplifications, BBMF finds a small set of interpretable bicliques linking patient subsets to recurrently co-altered chromosomal arms, providing a compact, biologically meaningful summary of tumor heterogeneity and demonstrating BBMF's utility for uncovering discrete latent structure in complex binary data.

</details>


### [164] [Geometrical fairness in graph neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17684)
*Arturo Pérez-Peralta,Sandra Benítez-Peña,Blas Kolic,Rosa E. Lillo*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图扩散的公平性感知学习方法，通过修改图的拉普拉斯算子——包括子空间投影、频谱调整和频率滤波等多种互补变换——来抑制数据中的偏差成分，在保持竞争性性能的同时提升公平性指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于扩散过程的图学习框架统一并扩展了传统图神经网络，但它们可能传播甚至放大数据中已有的偏见，因此亟需在图扩散框架中引入公平性机制。

Method: 通过修改图扩散过程中底层的拉普拉斯算子，融合子空间投影、频谱调整和频率滤波等多种互补变换，定向削弱与偏差相关的成分，并利用图扩散的固有平滑特性进行理论分析。

Result: 在合成数据集和真实数据集上的评估表明，该方法在有限额外计算开销下，取得了竞争性的性能表现和公平性指标的提升。

Conclusion: 该方法有效实现了图扩散框架下的公平性增强，在维持模型性能的同时显著降低了偏见传播，具有实用价值和理论支撑。

Abstract: Graph-based learning methods have become increasingly prominent due to their strong performance across diverse applications. Among these, recent frameworks grounded in diffusion processes provide a unifying perspective that extends traditional graph neural network formulations while addressing limitations of standard message-passing mechanisms. Despite these advances, concerns remain regarding the fairness of such models, as they may propagate or amplify biases present in the data. In this work, we introduce a fairness-aware adaptation of graph-based diffusion by modifying the underlying Laplacian operator. Our approach incorporates multiple complementary transformations, including subspace projections, spectral adjustments, and frequency-based filtering, to mitigate bias-related components. Leveraging the intrinsic smoothing properties of graph diffusion, we provide a principled analysis of the resulting behavior and establish theoretical insights into fairness properties. We evaluate the proposed framework on both synthetic and real-world datasets, demonstrating that it achieves competitive performance while improving fairness metrics with limited additional computational cost.

</details>


### [165] [Differential Privacy of Gaussian Process Posterior Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17995)
*Tomasz Maciazek*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了在训练集（含协变量与响应）完全私密的情况下，从高斯过程（GP）释放后验样本路径的隐私性。作者证明后验采样的内在随机性本身即可提供Rényi差分隐私（DP）保证，并推导了显式的隐私界，揭示了后验均值泄漏与数据依赖的后验协方差泄漏的分离，表明有效隐私强度与脊正则化密切相关。通过成员推断攻击和下游任务实验验证了理论分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统差分隐私机制依赖向输出添加外部噪声，而高斯过程后验采样天然具有随机性。作者希望探索这种内在随机性是否以及在多大程度上能够提供形式化的隐私保证，从而避免或减少外部噪声注入，在保护隐私的同时更好地保留数据效用。

Method: （1）对GP后验样本路径释放推导显式Rényi-DP界，将隐私泄漏分解为后验均值泄漏（由脊正则化强度控制）和数据依赖的后验协方差泄漏两部分；（2）使用成员推断攻击（membership-inference attacks）对理论泄漏进行实证验证；（3）通过下游后验采样任务的效用实验，识别隐私兼容正则化可保留有效决策的噪声观测区间，并展示可通过添加校准GP噪声作为额外的隐私调节旋钮。

Result: （1）后验样本路径释放的内禀随机性可提供非平凡的DP保证；（2）隐私泄漏的理论界与正则化强度、后验方差及释放的样本路径数量存在明确的依赖关系；（3）成员推断攻击的实证泄漏与理论预测一致；（4）在噪声观测场景下，隐私兼容的正则化设定可在适度效用损失下保留有效决策；需要更强隐私时，校准GP噪声可有效增强隐私保证。

Conclusion: GP后验采样的内在随机性本身就构成一种有意义的隐私保护机制，其隐私强度可通过脊正则化、后验样本路径数量以及可选的校准噪声注入进行精细化控制。这为构建无需外部噪声或仅需少量额外噪声的隐私保护高斯过程方法奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: We study the privacy of releasing posterior sample paths from a Gaussian process (GP) when the entire training set including covariates and responses is private. Unlike standard differential-privacy (DP) mechanisms that add external noise, posterior sampling is random by construction. We show that this intrinsic randomness yields DP guarantees by deriving explicit Rényi-DP bounds for GP posterior sample-path release. The bounds separate posterior-mean leakage from data-dependent posterior-covariance leakage showing that meaningful privacy depends sharply on effective ridge regularisation. We apply membership-inference attacks to show that empirical leakage follows the predicted dependence on regularisation, posterior variance and the number of released posterior sample-paths. Utility experiments on downstream posterior-sampling tasks identify noisy-observation regimes where privacy-compatible regularisation preserves useful decisions with modest utility loss. When stronger privacy is needed, the intrinsic guarantee can be sharpened by adding calibrated GP noise, providing an explicit additional privacy knob.

</details>


### [166] [Fast Nonparametric Conditional Independence Testing via Two-Stage Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18011)
*Eric V. Strobl*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: BLITZ是一种非参数条件独立性检验方法，采用"从宽到局部"的两阶段残差化策略：先用低阶多项式回归移除条件集的宽泛平滑依赖，再用浅层树回归处理非线性特征残差，以残差互协方差作为检验统计量，并通过矩匹配卡方近似零分布。该方法在保持高速度的同时实现了良好的校准性，适用于约束型因果发现中数千次条件独立性检验的需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约束型因果发现算法需要执行大量条件独立性检验，现有快速非参数检验方法往往在校准性上做出牺牲，尤其当变量与条件集之间存在非线性依赖关系时。研究者旨在设计一种既能在秒级以下完成检验、又能保持足够统计准确性的方法，以满足因果发现中数千次查询的实际需求。

Method: 提出BLITZ（Broad-to-Local Independence Testing via residualiZation）方法：第一阶段用低阶多项式回归移除条件集的宽泛平滑依赖；第二阶段对残差施加小型非线性特征映射，再用浅层树回归对这些特征进行残差化；最终以残差互协方差构建检验统计量，采用矩匹配卡方分布近似零分布。理论上证明了两阶段设计降低了树残差化器面临的等效复杂度，使浅层树能在控制残差条件均值偏差的同时避免过度过拟合。

Result: 模拟实验中，BLITZ的零假设校准性优于快速核方法、随机特征方法和基于回归的竞争方法，同时保持在最快方法之列。在合成图和流式细胞术数据的因果发现实验中，BLITZ在保留的邻接关系中产生了更可靠的端点定向，结构恢复也具有竞争力。

Conclusion: "从宽到局部"的残差化策略为实现校准良好、可扩展的非参数条件独立性检验提供了可行路径，适用于大规模因果发现任务。

Abstract: Constraint-based causal discovery relies on repeated conditional independence tests, but fast nonparametric tests often sacrifice calibration, especially when variables depend on the conditioning set through nonlinear relationships. We introduce BLITZ (Broad-to-Local Independence Testing via residualiZation), a nonparametric conditional independence test designed to run well under a second while maintaining the accuracy needed for the thousands of queries performed by constraint-based causal discovery algorithms. BLITZ first removes broad smooth dependence on the conditioning set using low-order polynomial regression, then applies a small nonlinear feature map and residualizes those features with shallow tree regressions. The resulting statistic tests residual cross-covariance, with a moment-matched chi-square approximation to the null distribution. We show theoretically that the two-stage design reduces the effective complexity faced by the tree residualizers, allowing shallow trees to control residual conditional-mean bias while avoiding excessive overfitting. In simulations, BLITZ provides better null calibration than fast kernel, random-feature, and regression-based competitors while remaining among the fastest methods tested. In causal discovery experiments on synthetic graphs and flow-cytometry data, BLITZ yields more reliable endpoint orientations among retained adjacencies and competitive structural recovery. These results suggest that broad-to-local residualization is a practical route to calibrated, scalable nonparametric conditional independence testing for causal discovery.

</details>


### [167] [Tensor-based second-order causal discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18074)
*Nathan Ouyang,Kexin Wan,Anna Seigal*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: TSCD 是一种基于张量的因果发现算法，利用观测数据和干预数据的协方差矩阵，在线性结构方程模型（DAG）下恢复因果图和边上的函数，仅要求噪声不相关，且干预数量随变量数呈对数增长，可扩展至数百个变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果发现方法在高阶矩统计效率低、一阶矩不可识别、对高斯性敏感等方面存在局限。二阶统计量（协方差矩阵）兼具统计与计算效率、可识别性，且对高斯和非高斯变量均适用，因此作者聚焦于利用二阶统计量进行因果发现。

Method: 提出 TSCD 算法：将观测数据和干预数据的协方差矩阵构造成张量作为输入；在线性 SEM 和 DAG 假设下，利用张量结构中的代数约束恢复因果顺序和边上的线性函数；仅要求噪声变量不相关；同时实现了非线性模型的扩展版本。

Result: 理论上证明了 TSCD 在干预数量仅为变量数对数级别时即可实现因果顺序和参数的可识别性；实验表明 TSCD 对噪声鲁棒、性能与现有方法相当，且可扩展至数百个变量。

Conclusion: TSCD 通过巧妙地利用二阶统计量和张量表示，在保持统计和计算效率的同时，实现了对数级别干预数量下的可识别因果发现，展示了其在大规模因果发现中的潜力。

Abstract: Causal discovery seeks to uncover the causal dependencies among variables. For this purpose, we propose an algorithm called Tensor-based Second-order Causal Discovery (TSCD). Its input is a tensor obtained from the covariance matrices of observational and interventional data. Assuming the causal dependencies follow a linear structural equation model on a directed acyclic graph (DAG), TSCD outputs the DAG and the functions on its edges, requiring only that the noise variables are uncorrelated. We also implement a version of the approach for nonlinear models. Our focus on second-order statistics (via the covariance matrices) is motivated by their statistical and computational efficiency relative to higher-order moments, their identifiability relative to first-order statistics, and that they work regardless of whether the variables are Gaussian. We show that TSCD has identifiable causal order and parameters from a number of interventions that is logarithmic in the number of variables. Experiments show that TSCD is robust to noise, competitive with existing methods, and scales to hundreds of variables.

</details>


### [168] [A Diffusion Approximation for Temporal-Difference Learning with Linear Features under Markovian Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18183)
*M. Forzo,E. Monzio Compagnoni,A. Russo,A. Pacchiano*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文引入随机微分方程（SDE）来近似线性TD(0)学习，弥补了经典ODE描述仅捕捉渐近均值动态而忽略随机涨落的不足。该SDE模型将投影Bellman算子的收缩动力学与Markov采样噪声分离开来，从而解释了恒定步长下误差地板的形成机制——源于Markov长期协方差与投影Bellman算子收缩几何之间的相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的线性TD学习连续时间描述采用常微分方程（ODE），虽能刻画渐近均值动态，却忽视了决定误差地板的随机涨落。因此，需要一种能够同时捕获收缩动力学和Markov采样噪声影响的更精细建模方法。

Method: 作者针对线性TD(0)在Markov噪声下的情形，引入随机微分方程（SDE）作为近似模型。该SDE将动态分解为两部分：（1）投影Bellman算子驱动的主收缩项；（2）反映Markov采样噪声的扩散项，其强度由Markov长期协方差刻画。

Result: 所得SDE模型成功区分了投影Bellman算子的收缩动力学与Markov采样的噪声贡献。基于此，作者给出了恒定步长下误差地板的解析解释：误差地板由Markov链的长期协方差与投影Bellman算子收缩几何的相互作用共同决定。

Conclusion: SDE近似为线性TD学习提供了一种比经典ODE更精细的分析工具，能够有效刻画有限步长下的波动行为，并揭示误差地板的本质来源。这为理解TD学习在Markov噪声下的有限样本行为提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: Temporal difference (TD) learning with linear function approximation is a core method for policy evaluation. Its classical continuous-time description is an ordinary differential equation (ODE), which captures the asymptotic mean dynamics but neglects stochastic fluctuations determining the error floor. We introduce a stochastic differential equation (SDE) approximation for linear TD(0) under Markovian noise. The resulting model distinguishes the contraction dynamics governed by the projected Bellman operator from the influence of Markovian sampling. As a consequence, the model explains the constant-stepsize error floor through the interaction between Markovian long-run covariance and the contraction geometry of the projected Bellman operator.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [169] [Correct When Paired, Wrong When Split: Decoupling and Editing Modality-Specific Neurons in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17057)
*Tingchao Fu,Wenkai Wang,Fanxiao Li,Huadong Zhang,Jinhong Zhang,Dayang Li,Yunyun Dong,Renyang Liu,Wei Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）中的编辑解耦失败问题，即通过多模态输入更新的知识在单模态输入下会回退到旧事实，原因是知识以解耦的模态特异性通路存储。作者提出DECODE方法，通过显式解耦并定位模态特异性神经元群来实现跨模态一致的知识编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前MLLMs的知识编辑范式存在一个关键但未被充分探索的问题：当使用文本-图像多模态查询对更新实体知识后，模型在多模态触发下表现良好，但将输入拆分为单模态（纯文本或纯图像）时，模型会回退到编辑前的旧知识。这一现象的根本原因和解决方案尚未被系统研究。

Method: 提出DECODE方法，核心思路是：(1) 通过实证分析揭示MLLMs中的实体知识并非以统一表征存储，而是分布在解耦的模态特异性通路中；(2) 显式解耦并定位面向目标知识的模态特异性神经元群；(3) 针对这些神经元群进行精准编辑，确保更新能有效传播到单模态通路，从而桥接模态间的编辑鸿沟。

Result: 大量实验表明，DECODE能够在不同模态触发条件下（多模态和单模态输入）一致地实现有效的知识更新，显著缓解了编辑解耦失败问题，在多个基准测试上取得了一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: MLLMs中的知识编辑面临编辑解耦失败的挑战，根源在于模态特异性知识存储机制。DECODE通过解耦定位模态特异性神经元群，实现了跨模态一致的知识更新，为多模态模型的知识编辑提供了新的视角和有效解决方案。

Abstract: Although Knowledge Editing provides an efficient mechanism for updating the knowledge of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), we find that current paradigms still suffer from an important yet remain underexplored issue : editing decoupling failure, where entity-related knowledge can be updated when the model is triggered by multimodal inputs (text--image query pairs), however, it often reverts to outdated pre-edit facts when the paired inputs are split into unimodal ones. Our in-depth empirical analysis reveals that the entity knowledge in MLLMs is not stored as a unified representation, but is instead distributed across disentangled modality-specific pathways. As a result, updates biased toward multimodal queries fail to propagate effectively to unimodal circuits. To bridge this gap, we propose DECODE, which explicitly disentangles and localizes modality-specific neuron groups for targeted knowledge. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DECODE consistently achieves effective knowledge updates under different modality triggers, thereby mitigating editing decoupling failures.

</details>


### [170] [Diagnosing and Repairing Shape-Prior Shortcuts in Long-Range Single-Shot Fringe Projection Profilometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17093)
*Adam Haroon,Anush Lakshman,Cody Fleming,Beiwen Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PhiCalNet架构，通过输出包裹相位而非深度并结合可微分标定层，从架构层面消除形状先验捷径，将单帧远距FPP的物体MAE降低3.3倍；同时利用机制可解释性与共形不确定性量化收敛诊断同一失效点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远距（>1m）单帧FPP因平方反比光衰导致条纹信噪比低、单图缺失条纹级次信息使问题不适定，且现有学习方法的架构机理未被系统研究。基线UNet依赖物体边界形状先验而非条纹相位解码来求解。

Method: 构建真实感合成基准（15600张条纹图，50个物体，1.5-2.1m）。使用三种探针（线性探测、Grad-CAM、平面外分布测试）进行机制可解释性诊断，发现基线捷径；提出PhiCalNet——输出包裹相位后接固定可微分标定层映射至深度，从架构假设空间中移除形状先验解；辅以像素级共形UQ进行验证。

Result: 基线UNet物体MAE为14.54mm。PhiCalNet将物体MAE降至4.46mm（3.3倍提升），残余误差集中在±π包裹跳变处（0.103%像素）。共形UQ剔除5%高不一致像素后，PhiCalNet RMSE降低64%（20.6→7.4mm），而基线仅降3.5%。MI与UQ收敛于同一失效位置。

Conclusion: 架构层面的修复（约束假设空间）比损失函数软惩罚更有效；机制可解释性与不确定性量化可作为收敛诊断工具相互印证；远距单帧FPP的核心残余挑战在于包裹相位跳变处。

Abstract: Learning-based single-shot fringe projection profilometry (FPP) has been studied mostly at close range. The long-range regime (standoff beyond 1 m) remains largely unaddressed: inverse-square intensity falloff lowers fringe signal-to-noise ratio and degrades physical ground truth, the single-shot problem is ill-posed because fringe-order information is absent from one image, and these architectures have not been studied mechanistically. We present a diagnose-repair-verify study using mechanistic interpretability (MI) and conformal uncertainty quantification (UQ) as convergent diagnostics: they agree on one physical failure locus, driving and verifying an architectural repair. On a photorealistic synthetic benchmark (15,600 fringe images, 50 objects at 1.5-2.1 m), a best UNet baseline reaches 14.54 mm object mean absolute error (MAE). Three probes (linear probing, Grad-CAM, flat-plane out-of-distribution test) converge: the baseline solves the task via object-boundary shape priors rather than fringe-phase decoding. We repair this with PhiCalNet, which outputs wrapped phase rather than depth and applies a fixed differentiable calibration layer mapping phase to depth, removing the shape-prior solution from the hypothesis space architecturally rather than by a loss penalty. A physics-informed loss that enforces the same physics as a soft penalty on a depth-regressing network yields no measurable gain, isolating the architecture as the operative factor. PhiCalNet reduces object MAE 3.3x to 4.46 mm; the residual is carried by 0.103% of pixels at the +/-pi wrap discontinuity. Pixel-wise conformal UQ confirms the diagnosis: rejecting the top 5% of object pixels by snapshot disagreement cuts PhiCalNet RMSE by 64% (20.6->7.4 mm) versus 3.5% for the baseline. MI and UQ converge on the same failure locus.

</details>


### [171] [Informative Missingness to Generate Irregular Clinical Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17106)
*Hadi Mehdizavareh,Gabriele Santangelo,Giovanna Nicora,Simon Lebech Cichosz,Arianna Dagliati,Arijit Khan,Riccardo Bellazzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于扩散模型的方法，用于生成联合建模实验室检测值及其缺失（观察）模式的临床时间序列。该方法扩展了TimeDiff框架，通过互补的扩散目标同时学习连续的检测值和离散的缺失模式，在MIMIC-III衍生的DACMI基准上验证了生成数据与真实患者轨迹的高度一致性，展示了在MNAR缺失下捕获临床决策与患者生理之间依赖关系的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子健康记录中的实验室检测是不定期采集的，检测缺失本身蕴含临床医生决策和患者生理状态的重要信息（MNAR机制）。传统方法将缺失视为预处理伪影，而本研究主张直接建模缺失模式，以保留数据中蕴含的临床意义。

Method: (1) 数据预处理：将图表时间对齐到4小时间隔，将入院记录分割为7天窗口，每个检测值与对应的观察指示器配对，应用标准化和归一化。(2) 模型扩展：基于TimeDiff扩散框架，设计互补的扩散目标，同时学习连续检测值和离散缺失模式。(3) 在MIMIC-III衍生的DACMI公开基准上进行实验验证，评估生成数据在单个检测分布和联合值-缺失嵌入上的保真度。

Result: 生成的合成数据在个体实验室检测分布和联合值-缺失嵌入两个维度上均与真实患者轨迹高度匹配，表明扩散模型能够有效捕获MNAR缺失下患者生理与临床检测行为之间的临床有意义的依赖关系。

Conclusion: 该模型可作为构建临床基础模型的初始组件。通过生成保留关键生理-缺失关系的合成先验，这项工作为后续训练能够利用信息性缺失的Prior-Data Fitted Networks（PFN）奠定了基础，扩展工作将进一步探索。

Abstract: Laboratory tests in electronic health records are collected irregularly, and the absence of a test order can be as informative as the measurement itself. Such missingness reflects clinicians' decisions and patient physiology, making it important to model it directly rather than treat it as a preprocessing artifact. Here we present a diffusion-based approach for generating clinical time series that jointly models laboratory values and their observation patterns using the public Data Analytics Challenge on Missing Data Imputation (DACMI) benchmark derived from MIMIC-III. To preserve realistic sampling, we align chart times into 4-hour intervals and segment admissions into 7-day windows, producing trajectories that pair each lab value with a corresponding observation indicator. Standard transformations and normalization are applied to stabilize training.
